Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on tissue structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 440. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 930 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to tissue structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 440/930 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Given a tissue algebra A over field F with optimality condition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 292. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 970/3.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
